  • 本期埃博拉病毒类群研究集中呈现以下进展。

  • 历史报告记录了埃博拉病毒病 暴发 期间的重大孕产妇死亡、流产、死产和新生儿 暴发,但大部分证据基于小病例系列、选定的有症状人群和 暴发 特定研究。由于证据基础的异质性,这些风险的程度仍不清楚。 [PMCID:PMC13696912]。

  • 截至 2026 年 8 月 7 日至 8 日,伊图里在 36 个卫生区中的 28 个卫生区报告了 3,636 例确诊病例和 1,551 例死亡(病死率 42.7%),而北基伍在 34 个卫生区中的 12 个报告了 470 例病例和 315 例死亡(病死率 67.0%)。北基伍省的接触者追踪后续行动(74.7%)低于全国数字(79.2%)和 90-95% 的中断阈值。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Seven compounds significantly reduced virus titers at least 100-fold with minimal cytotoxicity and demonstrated low IC 50 values. [10.1016/j.virusres.2026.199823].
  • Historical reports have documented significant maternal death, pregnancy loss, stillbirth, and newborn mortality during EVD outbreaks, but much of this evidence is based on small case series, selected symptomatic populations, and outbreak-specific studies.
  • The study revealed the molecular basis for EBOV-515 cross-reactivity and identified several conserved epitopes in the GP that are targets for cross-reactive antibodies. [10.64898/2026.10.22.761020].
  • As of 7–8 August 2026, Ituri reported 3,636 confirmed cases and 1,551 deaths (case fatality 42.7%) across 28 of 36 health zones, while North Kivu reported 470 cases and 315 deaths (case fatality 67.0%) across 12 of 34 health zones.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Virus research  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-25  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.virusres.2026.199823  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 373, 199823

利用生物病毒系统鉴定 埃博拉病毒 的小分子抑制剂。

Identification of small molecule inhibitors of Ebola virus by use of a biologically contained virus system.
作者： Meareg G. Amare, Peter J. Halfmann
摘要中文翻译埃博拉病毒病 是一种 死亡率 较高的严重疾病,可用的治疗选择有限。识别针对病毒蛋白的新 抗病毒药物 药物对于改善预防和 治疗 策略至关重要。在这项研究中,我们筛选了一个包含 2,620 个小分子的文库,这些小分子富含报道的针对细胞和病毒解旋酶的抑制活性,以鉴定稳定表达 VP30 蛋白的 Vero 细胞中生物包含的病毒(埃博拉ΔVP30 病毒)的表型抑制剂。七种化合物显着降低病毒滴度至少 100 倍,且细胞毒性最小,并且 IC 50 值较低。其中两种化合物完全抑制埃博拉ΔVP30病毒感染,干扰病毒进入或复制。尽管这些初步筛选的命中需要进一步优化和体内验证,但它们的鉴定为抗 埃博拉病毒 药物发现开辟了新途径。

Identification of small molecule inhibitors of Ebola virus by use of a biologically contained virus system.

Authors: Meareg G. Amare, Peter J. Halfmann
Original abstract (English)
Ebola virus disease is a severe illness with high mortality for which limited therapeutic options are available. Identifying new antiviral drugs that target viral proteins is essential for improving prevention and treatment strategies. In this study, we screened a library of 2,620 small molecules enriched for reported inhibitory activity against cellular and viral helicases to identify phenotypic inhibitors of a biologically contained virus, Ebola∆VP30 virus, in Vero cells stably expressing VP30 protein. Seven compounds significantly reduced virus titers at least 100-fold with minimal cytotoxicity and demonstrated low IC 50 values. Two of these compounds completely inhibited EbolaΔVP30 virus infection, interfering with either virus entry or replication. Although these preliminary screening hits require further optimization and in vivo validation, their identification opens new avenues for anti-Ebola virus drug discovery.

Core methods

Interpretation and novelty

Limitations and evidence strength

2026 Bundibugyo 病毒糖蛋白的结构和抗原性

Structure and antigenicity of the 2026 Bundibugyo virus glycoprotein
作者： Amin Addetia, Keiya Uriu, Cameron Stewart, Kaitlin R. Sprouse, Yu Kaku, Mohammad Golam Kibria, Olivia Putri, SM Tanjil Shah, Yuri Fukushima, Shushuke Kawakubo
摘要中文翻译由一种新的人畜共患本迪布焦病毒(BDBV)跨物种溢出引起的埃博拉病毒病暴发目前正在刚果民主共和国和乌干达以前所未有的速度传播。暴发 已导致 7,733 例病例,病死率为 48%,但尚无 BDBV 特异性 疫苗 或治疗方法。埃博拉病毒糖蛋白 (GP) 促进病毒进入,是中和抗体和 疫苗 开发的目标。在这里,我们从当代 BDBV 分离株中设计了一种可溶性、融合前稳定的 GP 三聚体,并确定了其与广泛中和抗体 EBOV-515 结合的冷冻电子显微镜结构。它揭示了 EBOV-515 交叉反应性的分子基础,并表明该抗体除了将 GP 捕获在融合前构象并阻碍聚糖帽裂解之外,还通过干扰 NPC1 接合来抑制病毒进入。谱系定义取代图谱确定了 GP1 和 GP2 的变化,包括识别融合环的多种广泛中和抗体靶向的抗原位点内的 R506S。尽管如此,我们发现几种聚糖帽、GP 碱基和 HR2 定向抗体交叉反应并中和 BDBV DRC/2026 GP 假病毒,其效力与针对早期 BDBV 分离株和扎伊尔埃博拉病毒 Makona/2024 分离株观察到的效力相当或更强。这些发现定义了当代 BDBV GP 的抗原特性,并将保守表位描述为抗体治疗和泛埃博拉病毒 疫苗 开发的理想靶标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于正在进行的 暴发 且缺乏特定的 治疗 或 疫苗,需要进行这项研究来了解 2026 本迪布焦病毒糖蛋白 (GP) 的结构和抗原性,以便为 疫苗 和治疗开发提供信息。
设计与对象
该研究涉及从当代 BDBV 分离株中工程化可溶性、融合前稳定的 GP 三聚体,并通过冷冻电子显微镜确定其结构,与广泛中和抗体 EBOV-515 结合。
核心方法
这些方法包括 BDBV GP 三聚体的生成、冷冻电子显微镜结构测定和抗体结合分析。
主要结果
该研究揭示了 EBOV-515 交叉反应性的分子基础,并鉴定了 GP 中的几个保守表位,这些表位是交叉反应性抗体的目标。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,抗体 EBOV-515 通过干扰 NPC1 结合并将 GP 捕获在融合前构象中来抑制病毒进入,并且几种抗体可以与早期分离株发生交叉反应并中和 BDBV。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现定义了当代 BDBV GP 的抗原特性,并将保守表位描述为抗体治疗和泛埃博拉病毒 疫苗 的理想靶标。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到单一当代 BDBV 分离株的使用以及需要在不同分离株和环境中进一步验证的限制。

Structure and antigenicity of the 2026 Bundibugyo virus glycoprotein

Authors: Amin Addetia, Keiya Uriu, Cameron Stewart, Kaitlin R. Sprouse, Yu Kaku, Mohammad Golam Kibria, Olivia Putri, SM Tanjil Shah, Yuri Fukushima, Shushuke Kawakubo
Original abstract (English)
An outbreak of ebolavirus disease caused by a new zoonotic spillover of Bundibugyo virus (BDBV) is currently spreading at an unprecedented pace in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda. The outbreak has resulted in 7,733 cases with a case-fatality ratio of 48%, yet no BDBV-specific vaccines or therapeutics are available. The ebolavirus glycoprotein (GP) promotes viral entry and is the target of neutralizing antibodies and vaccine development. Here, we engineered a soluble, prefusion-stabilized GP trimer from a contemporary BDBV isolate and determined its cryo-electron microscopy structure bound to the broadly neutralizing antibody EBOV-515. It reveals the molecular basis for EBOV-515 cross-reactivity and suggests that the antibody inhibits viral entry by interfering with NPC1 engagement in addition to trapping GP in the prefusion conformation and hindering glycan cap cleavage. Mapping lineage-defining substitutions identified changes in GP1 and GP2, including R506S within an antigenic site targeted by multiple broadly neutralizing antibodies recognizing the fusion loop. Nevertheless, we found that several glycan cap-, GP base-, and HR2-directed antibodies cross-reacted and neutralized BDBV DRC/2026 GP pseudovirus with potency comparable or greater to that observed against earlier BDBV isolates and the Zaire ebolavirus Makona/2024 isolate. These findings define the antigenic properties of contemporary BDBV GP and delineate conserved epitopes as ideal targets for antibody therapeutics and for the development of pan-ebolavirus vaccines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to understand the structure and antigenicity of the 2026 Bundibugyo virus glycoprotein (GP) to inform vaccine and therapeutic development, given the ongoing outbreak and lack of specific treatments or vaccines.
Design and population
The study involved the engineering of a soluble, prefusion-stabilized GP trimer from a contemporary BDBV isolate and its cryo-electron microscopy structure determination, bound to the broadly neutralizing antibody EBOV-515.
Core methods
The methods included the generation of a BDBV GP trimer, cryo-electron microscopy structure determination, and antibody binding analysis.
Main results
The study revealed the molecular basis for EBOV-515 cross-reactivity and identified several conserved epitopes in the GP that are targets for cross-reactive antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the antibody EBOV-515 inhibits viral entry by interfering with NPC1 engagement and trapping GP in the prefusion conformation, and that several antibodies cross-react and neutralize BDBV with potency comparable to earlier isolates.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings define the antigenic properties of contemporary BDBV GP and delineate conserved epitopes as ideal targets for antibody therapeutics and pan-ebolavirus vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a single contemporary BDBV isolate and the need for further validation in different isolates and settings.

争议地区的流行病应对表现:2026年本迪布焦病毒病期间伊图里和北基伍省的比较分析 暴发

Epidemic response performance in contested territory: a comparative analysis of Ituri and North Kivu during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak
作者： Marie Roseline Darnycka Belizaire, Steve Ahuca, II YB, Otim Patrick Cossy Ramadan, Ebenezer Obi Daniel, Dick Chamla, Aliou Diallo, Solomon Fisseha Woldetsadik, Etien Luc Koua, Ishata Nannie Conteh
摘要中文翻译抽象背景。刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 的 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 (BVD) 暴发 正在各省蔓延,治理和安全条件截然不同。伊图里是 暴发 的震中,尽管不安全,但仍受国家政府管理;自 2025 年初以来,北基伍省首府戈马及周边地区一直处于 3 月 23 日运动 (M23)/刚果河流联盟 (AFC) 的控制之下。现有的公开报道将不安全视为一般的、无差别的风险因素,而不是孤立有争议的领土控制的具体贡献。我们使用世卫组织、非洲疾病预防控制中心和非洲疾病预防控制中心公布的按省分列的 监测 数据,比较了伊图里和北基伍的累计病例死亡率、卫生区覆盖率和接触者追踪完整性,并结合有记录的刚果第十次埃博拉病毒 暴发(2018-2020 年)与冲突相关的破坏(该事件影响了这两个省)。使用具有 95% 置信区间的二比例 z 检验来测试观察到的差异是否超过单独机会产生的差异;该分析自始至终都是假设生成,与生态设计的限制相一致。截至 2026 年 8 月 7 日至 8 日,伊图里在 36 个卫生区中的 28 个卫生区报告了 3,636 例确诊病例和 1,551 例死亡(病死率 42.7%);北基伍省 34 个卫生区中的 12 个卫生区报告了 470 例病例和 315 例死亡(病死率 67.0%)。北基伍省的接触者追踪后续行动(74.7%)低于全国数字(79.2%)和 90-95% 的中断阈值。暴发 在响应激活后 40 天内达到 1,000 例确诊病例,而 2018 年 暴发 在同一地区大约需要 235 天。北基伍-伊图里病死率差距(24.4 个百分点;95% CI 19.8–28.9;z = 9.98,p
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估刚果民主共和国 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 期间,有争议的领土控制对流行病应对表现的影响。
设计与对象
该研究比较了伊图里省和北基伍省在暴发期间处于不同治理和安全条件下的疫情应对表现。伊图里仍处于国家政府管理之下,而北基伍省首府及周边地区自 2025 年初以来处于“3·23”运动/AFC 的控制之下。
核心方法
研究人员使用世界卫生组织、欧洲疾病预防控制中心和非洲疾病预防控制中心公布的按省分列的 监测 数据来比较伊图里和北基伍之间的累计病例死亡率、卫生区覆盖率和接触者追踪完整性。他们应用置信区间为 95% 的二比例 z 检验来评估观察到的差异是否具有统计显着性。
主要结果
截至 2026 年 8 月 7 日至 8 日,伊图里在 36 个卫生区中的 28 个卫生区报告了 3,636 例确诊病例和 1,551 例死亡(病死率 42.7%),而北基伍在 34 个卫生区中的 12 个报告了 470 例病例和 315 例死亡(病死率 67.0%)。北基伍省的接触者追踪后续行动(74.7%)低于全国数字(79.2%)和 90-95% 的中断阈值。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解读为强调了治理和安全条件对疫情应对有效性的重大影响,并指出两省之间存在显着的病死率差距。该分析的新颖之处在于它关注有争议的领土控制对 暴发 动态的具体贡献。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,在治理和安全环境不同的地区,特别是受冲突影响的地区,需要采取量身定制的流行病应对方法,从而为公共卫生战略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其生态设计和对已发布的 监测 数据的依赖而受到限制,这些数据可能无法捕捉到当地应对工作的所有细微差别。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Epidemic response performance in contested territory: a comparative analysis of Ituri and North Kivu during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak

Authors: Marie Roseline Darnycka Belizaire, Steve Ahuca, II YB, Otim Patrick Cossy Ramadan, Ebenezer Obi Daniel, Dick Chamla, Aliou Diallo, Solomon Fisseha Woldetsadik, Etien Luc Koua, Ishata Nannie Conteh
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background. The 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) is unfolding across provinces under materially different governance and security conditions. Ituri, the outbreak's epicentre, remains under national government administration despite insecurity; North Kivu's capital, Goma, and surrounding territory have been under March 23 Movement (M23)/Alliance Fleuve Congo (AFC) control since early 2025. Existing published accounts treat insecurity as a generic, undifferentiated risk factor rather than isolating the specific contribution of contested territorial control. We compared Ituri and North Kivu on cumulative case fatality, health-zone coverage, and contact-tracing completeness using published, province-disaggregated surveillance figures from WHO, ECDC, and Africa CDC, contextualised against the documented conflict-related disruption of DRC's tenth Ebola outbreak (2018–2020), which affected the same two provinces. Two-proportion z-tests with 95% confidence intervals were used to test whether observed differences exceeded what chance alone would produce; the analysis is presented as hypothesis-generating throughout, consistent with the limits of an ecological design. As of 7–8 August 2026, Ituri had reported 3,636 confirmed cases and 1,551 deaths (case fatality 42.7%) across 28 of 36 health zones; North Kivu had reported 470 cases and 315 deaths (case fatality 67.0%) across 12 of 34 health zones. North Kivu's contact-tracing follow-up (74.7%) fell below both the national figure (79.2%) and the 90–95% interruption threshold. The outbreak reached 1,000 confirmed cases within 40 days of response activation, versus approximately 235 days for the 2018 outbreak in the same geography. The North Kivu-Ituri case-fatality gap (24.4 percentage points; 95% CI 19.8–28.9; z = 9.98, p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of contested territorial control on epidemic response performance during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.
Design and population
The study compared the epidemic response performance in Ituri and North Kivu provinces, which were under different governance and security conditions during the outbreak. Ituri remained under national government administration, while North Kivu's capital and surrounding areas were under M23/AFC control since early 2025.
Core methods
The researchers used published, province-disaggregated surveillance figures from WHO, ECDC, and Africa CDC to compare cumulative case fatality, health-zone coverage, and contact-tracing completeness between Ituri and North Kivu. They applied two-proportion z-tests with 95% confidence intervals to assess whether observed differences were statistically significant.
Main results
As of 7–8 August 2026, Ituri reported 3,636 confirmed cases and 1,551 deaths (case fatality 42.7%) across 28 of 36 health zones, while North Kivu reported 470 cases and 315 deaths (case fatality 67.0%) across 12 of 34 health zones. North Kivu's contact-tracing follow-up (74.7%) was below the national figure (79.2%) and the 90–95% interruption threshold.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the significant impact of governance and security conditions on epidemic response effectiveness, noting a marked case-fatality gap between the two provinces. This analysis is novel in its focus on the specific contribution of contested territorial control to outbreak dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform public health strategies by emphasizing the need for tailored epidemic response approaches in regions with varying governance and security contexts, particularly in conflict-affected areas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its ecological design and reliance on published surveillance figures, which may not capture all nuances of local response efforts. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these constraints.

希望 疫苗 对 Bundibugyo 病毒有效。

Hope for efficacious vaccines for Bundibugyo virus.
作者： The Lancet Infectious Diseases, The Lancet Infectious Diseases.
摘要中文翻译希望对 Bundibugyo 病毒有效的 疫苗 - PMID: 42906558 - DOI: 10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00535-9 希望对 Bundibugyo 病毒有效的 疫苗 出版物类型 LinkOut - 更多资源 - 全文来源 编辑 doi: 10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00535-9。剪贴板中的项目 柳叶刀感染疾病。没有可用的摘要
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究强调需要有效的 疫苗 来对抗本迪布焦病毒,因为它有可能引起严重的疾病,并且缺乏现有的治疗选择。目的是探索 疫苗 该病毒的开发可能性。
设计与对象
这篇社论讨论了围绕 Bundibugyo 病毒 疫苗 的研究前景,重点关注现有 疫苗 平台的潜力及其对该特定病毒的应用。该研究基于对现有文献和专家意见的回顾。
核心方法
该社论回顾了现有的 疫苗 研究,并评估了为其他 埃博拉病毒(例如苏丹病毒和扎伊尔埃博拉病毒)开发的 疫苗 平台适用于本迪布焦病毒的潜力。它没有描述具体的实验方法或统计分析。
主要结果
社论报道称,初步研究表明,可以使用为其他 埃博拉病毒 开发的 疫苗 平台来创建针对 Bundibugyo 病毒的有效 疫苗。但文中没有提供具体的结果或数据。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为针对 Bundibugyo 病毒的 疫苗 开发的一个有前途的途径,并指出现有平台可能适用。这代表了对跨病毒 疫苗 策略潜力的新视角。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,Bundibugyo 病毒的 疫苗 开发可以受益于现有平台的利用,这可能为预防该病毒 疫苗 的未来研究和公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于编辑评论,不提供原始数据或详细的实验结果。证据强度中等,因为它依赖于现有的研究和专家意见,而不是新的发现。

Hope for efficacious vaccines for Bundibugyo virus.

Authors: The Lancet Infectious Diseases, The Lancet Infectious Diseases.
Original abstract (English)
Hope for efficacious vaccines for Bundibugyo virus - PMID: 42906558 - DOI: 10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00535-9 Hope for efficacious vaccines for Bundibugyo virus Publication types LinkOut - more resources - Full Text Sources Editorial doi: 10.1016/S1473-3099(26)00535-9. Item in Clipboard Lancet Infect Dis. No abstract available
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study highlights the need for effective vaccines against Bundibugyo virus due to its potential to cause severe disease and the lack of existing therapeutic options. The objective is to explore vaccine development possibilities for this virus.
Design and population
This editorial discusses the research landscape surrounding vaccines for Bundibugyo virus, focusing on the potential of existing vaccine platforms and their application to this specific virus. The study is based on a review of available literature and expert opinion.
Core methods
The editorial reviews existing vaccine research and evaluates the potential of vaccine platforms developed for other Ebola viruses, such as Sudan virus and Zaire ebolavirus, to be adapted for Bundibugyo virus. It does not describe specific experimental methods or statistical analyses.
Main results
The editorial reports that preliminary studies suggest the possibility of using vaccine platforms developed for other Ebola viruses to create efficacious vaccines for Bundibugyo virus. However, no specific results or data are provided in the text.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a promising avenue for vaccine development against Bundibugyo virus, noting that existing platforms may be applicable. This represents a novel perspective on the potential for cross-virus vaccine strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that vaccine development for Bundibugyo virus could benefit from leveraging existing platforms, which may inform future research and public health strategies for preventing outbreaks of this virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an editorial review and does not present original data or detailed experimental results. The evidence strength is moderate, as it relies on existing research and expert opinion rather than new findings.

2015-2023 年刚果民主共和国病毒特异性 暴发 区域以外的丝状病毒血清反应性

Filovirus Seroreactivity Beyond Virus-Specific Outbreak Regions in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, 2015-2023
作者： Sydney Merritt, Jean Paul Kompany, P. Mukadi, Megan Halbrook, Kamy Musene, Michael Beya, Handdy Kalengi, Merly Tambu, John Daniel Kelly, Aquena H. Ball
摘要中文翻译摘要 刚果民主共和国(DRC)正在发生的本迪布焦病毒病(BVD)ZX​​QTERMXQXZ是有记录以来第二大埃博拉病毒病暴发,也是该国仅有第二记录的BVD 暴发。尽管刚果民主共和国已记录了 18 种丝状病毒疾病 暴发,但人类丝状病毒暴露的历史频率和地理分布仍知之甚少。使用多重多丝状病毒免疫测定,我们评估了 2015 年至 2023 年间来自七个省的 11 个 队列研究 中与人类疾病相关的丝状病毒、本迪布焦病毒 (BDBV)、埃博拉病毒 (EBOV)、马尔堡病毒 (MARV) 和苏丹病毒 (SUDV) 抗原的抗体反应性 (n=5,852)。荧光强度中值超过既定抗原特异性截止值的参与者被归类为血清反应性。在每个 队列研究 中均检测到所有四种病毒针对糖蛋白 (GP) 靶标的血清反应性,证明丝状病毒或抗原相关暴露的证据远远超出已知的 暴发 区域。血清反应性估计因 队列研究 站点而异,2016 年 4 月蒙加拉省 60.8% 的参与者 (n=175/288) 被认为对 BDBV GP 有血清反应性。在该 队列研究 中,分别有 4.2%、29.9% 和 34.7% 的参与者对 EBOV、MARV 和 SUDV GP 呈血清反应,但只有 1.7% 对所有四种 GP 抗原呈血清反应。在调整后的分析中,地点是血清反应性的主要预测因素,蒙加拉省和楚阿帕省的 BDBV 血清反应性几率比奎卢省高 9.1 倍(95% CI 7.0-11.9)和 9.5 倍(95% CI 6.9-13.2)。同样,Mongala 地区 MARV 和 SUDV GP 血清反应性的调整后赔率比 Kwilu 地区高 4.5 倍和 7.1 倍,Tshuapa 地区高 3.7 倍和 6.1 倍。在纵向采样的 280 名个体中,BDBV GP 检测到的血清转化率在不同地点具有可比性,奎卢 (Kwilu) 和蒙加拉 (Mongala) 分别有 12.8% 和 10.4% 在初次接触后三年和五年内检测到血清反应性。总体而言,这些结果表明 队列研究 样本中存在广泛的、地理异质的丝状病毒血清反应性。这些发现可能反映了人畜共患的 跨物种溢出 事件,导致未检测到的人际丝状病毒传播,包括少症状或无症状感染,或暴露于尚未鉴定的抗原交叉反应病毒。这些数据凸显了在了解刚果民主共和国丝状病毒暴露的历史和地理分布方面的差距,并支持进一步的前瞻性血清学、流行病学和分子研究,特别是考虑到当前的 暴发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
刚果民主共和国(DRC)正在发生的本迪布焦病毒病(BVD)ZX​​QTERMXQXZ是有记录以来第二大埃博拉病毒病暴发,也是该国仅有第二记录的BVD 暴发。尽管刚果民主共和国已记录了 18 种丝状病毒疾病 暴发,但人类丝状病毒暴露的历史频率和地理分布仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
使用多重多丝状病毒免疫测定,我们评估了 2015 年至 2023 年间来自七个省的 11 个 队列研究 中与人类疾病相关的丝状病毒、本迪布焦病毒 (BDBV)、埃博拉病毒 (EBOV)、马尔堡病毒 (MARV) 和苏丹病毒 (SUDV) 抗原的抗体反应性 (n=5,852)。
核心方法
荧光强度中值超过既定抗原特异性截止值的参与者被归类为血清反应性。血清反应性估计因 队列研究 站点而异,2016 年 4 月蒙加拉省 60.8% 的参与者 (n=175/288) 被认为对 BDBV GP 有血清反应性。在该 队列研究 中,分别有 4.2%、29.9% 和 34.7% 的参与者对 EBOV、MARV 和 SUDV GP 呈血清反应,但只有 1.7% 对所有四种 GP 抗原呈血清反应。
主要结果
在每个 队列研究 中均检测到所有四种病毒针对糖蛋白 (GP) 靶标的血清反应性,证明丝状病毒或抗原相关暴露的证据远远超出已知的 暴发 区域。在调整后的分析中,地点是血清反应性的主要预测因素,蒙加拉省和楚阿帕省的 BDBV 血清反应性几率比奎卢省高 9.1 倍(95% CI 7.0-11.9)和 9.5 倍(95% CI 6.9-13.2)。同样,Mongala 地区 MARV 和 SUDV GP 血清反应性的调整后赔率比 Kwilu 地区高 4.5 倍和 7.1 倍,Tshuapa 地区高 3.7 倍和 6.1 倍。
解释与创新
这些发现可能反映了人畜共患的 跨物种溢出 事件,导致未检测到的人际丝状病毒传播,包括少症状或无症状感染,或暴露于尚未鉴定的抗原交叉反应病毒。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果表明 队列研究 样本中存在广泛的、地理异质的丝状病毒血清反应性。这些数据凸显了在了解刚果民主共和国丝状病毒暴露的历史和地理分布方面的差距,并支持进一步的前瞻性血清学、流行病学和分子研究,特别是考虑到当前的 暴发。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据没有报告任何随机化或盲法,这可能会引入偏差。研究设计的局限性和缺乏比较组降低了证据的强度。

Filovirus Seroreactivity Beyond Virus-Specific Outbreak Regions in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, 2015-2023

Authors: Sydney Merritt, Jean Paul Kompany, P. Mukadi, Megan Halbrook, Kamy Musene, Michael Beya, Handdy Kalengi, Merly Tambu, John Daniel Kelly, Aquena H. Ball
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The ongoing Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) is the second-largest Ebola disease outbreak in recorded history, and only the second recorded BVD outbreak in the country. While the DRC has recorded 18 filovirus disease outbreaks, the historical frequency and geographic distribution of human filovirus exposures remain poorly understood. Using a multiplexed multi-filovirus immunoassay, we assessed antibody reactivity against antigens of filoviruses linked to human disease, Bundibugyo virus (BDBV), Ebola virus (EBOV), Marburg virus (MARV), and Sudan virus (SUDV), among eleven cohorts from seven provinces between 2015–2023 (n=5,852). Participants with median fluorescence intensity values exceeding established antigen-specific cutoffs were classified as seroreactive. Seroreactivity against glycoprotein (GP) targets of all four viruses were detected in every cohort, demonstrating evidence of filovirus or an antigenically related exposure well beyond known outbreak regions. Seroreactivity estimates varied by cohort site, with 60.8% of participants (n=175/288) in Mongala Province in April 2016 considered seroreactive against BDBV GP. In this cohort, 4.2%, 29.9%, and 34.7% of participants were seroreactive against EBOV, MARV and SUDV GP, respectively yet only 1.7% were seroreactive against all four GP antigens. In adjusted analyses, site was the primary predictor of seroreactivity, with odds for BDBV seroreactivity 9.1-fold (95%CI 7.0-11.9) and 9.5-fold (95%CI 6.9-13.2) higher in Mongala and Tshuapa provinces than in Kwilu. Similarly, the adjusted odds of MARV and SUDV GP seroreactivity were 4.5-fold and 7.1-fold higher in Mongala, and 3.7-fold and 6.1-fold higher in Tshuapa, than Kwilu. Among 280 individuals sampled longitudinally, BDBV GP detected seroconversion was comparable by site, with 12.8% in Kwilu and 10.4% in Mongala having detected seroreactivity three and five years after initial contact, respectively. Overall, these results indicate widespread, geographically heterogeneous filovirus seroreactivity across sampled cohorts. These findings may reflect zoonotic spillover events, leading to undetected human-to-human filovirus circulation, including paucisymptomatic or asymptomatic infections, or exposure to as-yet unidentified antigenically cross-reactive viruses. These data highlight gaps in understanding the historical and geographic distribution of filovirus exposure in the DRC and support further prospective serological, epidemiological, and molecular investigations, especially given the current outbreak.

Question and background
The ongoing Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) is the second-largest Ebola disease outbreak in recorded history, and only the second recorded BVD outbreak in the country. A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

埃博拉病毒病(EVD)与母婴健康:直接和间接影响及卫生系统准备不足

Ebola virus disease and maternal–neonatal health: direct and indirect impacts and health system preparedness gaps
作者： Ahmed N, Ibrahim F, Ali O
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨 埃博拉病毒病 对孕产妇-新生儿健康的直接和间接影响,并找出卫生系统准备方面的差距。这一背景凸显了埃博拉病毒病在撒哈拉以南非洲地区反复流行以及相关的高病死率,强调需要对孕产妇和新生儿结局进行有针对性的研究。
设计与对象
使用 PubMed、Scopus 和 Google Scholar 的综合搜索策略进行叙述性综述,重点关注与埃博拉病毒病、孕产妇健康、胎儿和新生儿结局以及卫生系统准备情况相关的文献。该研究包括观察性研究、暴发 报告、病例系列、模型研究和公共卫生指导。
核心方法
该评价采用叙述性方法,没有正式的系统评价方案或基于 PRISMA 的筛选过程。通过有针对性的搜索和参考列表确定文献,并根据其与埃博拉病毒病相关孕产妇、胎儿、新生儿、胎盘或妊娠相关结局的相关性选择出版物。通过主题比较以叙述方式综合数据,强调一致的模式和知识差距。
主要结果
历史报告记录了埃博拉病毒病 暴发 期间的重大孕产妇死亡、流产、死产和新生儿 暴发,但大部分证据基于小病例系列、选定的有症状人群和 暴发 特定研究。由于证据基础的异质性,这些风险的程度仍不清楚。
解释与创新
作者解释说,妊娠并发有症状的埃博拉病毒病与重大风险相关,但这些风险的严重程度尚不清楚。新颖之处在于强调需要对埃博拉病毒病期间的孕产妇-新生儿健康进行更有针对性的研究和监测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了将妊娠特异性药物警戒和母婴结局监测纳入 暴发 规划和应对的重要性。他们还强调,需要在埃博拉病毒病 暴发 期间保持孕产妇和新生儿服务的连续性,并改善孕产妇和新生儿健康的准备工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小、证据异构以及 暴发 临床管理差异的限制。证据强度中等,因为研究结果基于叙述综合而不是定量分析,并且数据主要来自观察性研究和 暴发 报告。

Ebola virus disease and maternal–neonatal health: direct and indirect impacts and health system preparedness gaps

Authors: Ahmed N, Ibrahim F, Ali O
Original abstract (English)
Pregnant women and neonates may experience serious adverse outcomes during outbreaks, yet pregnancy-specific evidence remains limited and maternal–neonatal health has historically received insufficient attention in Ebola preparedness and research.MethodsThis narrative review synthesized evidence on Ebola virus disease and maternal–neonatal health from peer-reviewed literature, international guidance, and outbreak reports. Findings were synthesized narratively, with attention to study design, methodological limitations, and differences across outbreak settings.FindingsAvailable evidence indicates that EVD during pregnancy is associated with severe maternal complications and poor fetal and neonatal outcomes, although risk estimates remain uncertain because of small and heterogeneous studies. 2Methodology2.1OverviewThis study employed a narrative review methodology guided by the Scale for the Assessment of Narrative Review Articles (SANRA) to enhance transparency, methodological rigor, and critical synthesis. The review examined the direct and indirect effects of Ebola virus disease (EVD) on maternal and neonatal health, including clinical outcomes, health-service disruption, and preparedness gaps. BackgroundEbola virus disease (EVD) is a severe viral infection that can substantially affect maternal and neonatal health through both direct disease and disruption of essential health services. Relevant literature was identified through targeted searches of PubMed, Scopus, and Google Scholar and through reference-list screening of key publications. Placental infection and mother-to-child transmission have been documented. Ebola outbreaks also disrupt antenatal, delivery, immunization, and emergency obstetric services, contributing to additional maternal and neonatal morbidity...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the direct and indirect impacts of Ebola virus disease on maternal–neonatal health and identify gaps in health system preparedness. The background highlights the recurring epidemics of EVD in sub-Saharan Africa and the associated high case fatality rates, emphasizing the need for targeted research on maternal and neonatal outcomes.
Design and population
A narrative review was conducted using a comprehensive search strategy across PubMed, Scopus, and Google Scholar, focusing on literature related to EVD, maternal health, fetal and neonatal outcomes, and health system preparedness. The study included observational studies, outbreak reports, case series, modeling studies, and public-health guidance.
Core methods
The review employed a narrative approach without a formal systematic-review protocol or PRISMA-based screening process. Literature was identified through targeted searches and reference lists, and publications were selected based on their relevance to maternal, fetal, neonatal, placental, or pregnancy-related outcomes associated with EVD. Data were synthesized narratively through thematic comparison, with emphasis on consistent patterns and knowledge gaps.
Main results
Historical reports have documented significant maternal death, pregnancy loss, stillbirth, and newborn mortality during EVD outbreaks, but much of this evidence is based on small case series, selected symptomatic populations, and outbreak-specific studies. The extent of these risks remains unclear due to the heterogeneity of the evidence base.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that pregnancies complicated by symptomatic EVD are associated with substantial risks, but the magnitude of these risks is not well understood. The novelty lies in highlighting the need for more focused research and surveillance on maternal–neonatal health during EVD outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of integrating pregnancy-specific pharmacovigilance and maternal–infant outcome monitoring into outbreak planning and response. They also emphasize the need for continuity of maternity and newborn services and improved preparedness for maternal–neonatal health during EVD outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the small sample sizes, heterogeneous evidence, and differences in clinical management across outbreaks. The evidence strength is moderate, as findings are based on narrative synthesis rather than quantitative analysis, and the data are primarily derived from observational studies and outbreak reports.

怀孕小鼠模型中 VSV-EBOV 疫苗 的免疫学和胎盘反应。

Immunologic and placental responses to the VSV-EBOV vaccine in a pregnant mouse model.
作者： Aaron P. Lewkowicz, Yi-Ru Wang, Mirian I Mendoza, Kaliroi Engel, Logan Kelley-Baker, Iara Rattner, Amber Lopez, Ian L. McWilliams, Derek Dc Ireland, Mohanraj Manangeeswaran
摘要中文翻译埃博拉病毒病 是一种严重的病毒性出血性疾病,孕产妇和围产儿 死亡率 发病率较高。尽管基于水泡性口炎病毒的埃博拉 疫苗 (VSV-EBOV) 已在 暴发 环境中成功部署,但孕妇被排除在临床试验之外,从而留下了严重的安全漏洞。为了解决这个问题,我们开发了一种小鼠模型来评估不同妊娠阶段暴露于 VSV-EBOV 后的妊娠结局。在胚胎第 7 天 (E7)、E10 或 E15 接种疫苗的怀孕 C57BL/6 小鼠产生了与非怀孕对照相当的强大抗 埃博拉病毒(抗 EBOV)抗体反应,并有效清除了 疫苗 病毒,而没有垂直 传播 的证据。然而,围植入期 疫苗接种 适度增加胎儿吸收和产后早期 死亡率,并伴有以血管重塑缺陷和炎症为特征的胎盘病理。妊娠后期 疫苗接种 对生存或后代发育的影响很小。机制研究表明,这些不良反应是由 I 型干扰素信号传导而非直接病毒感染介导的,因为 IFN-β 缺陷小鼠表现出正常妊娠结局,并且尽管病毒载量较高,但阻断干扰素受体活性可改善胎盘病理。据我们所知,这些研究结果代表了 VSV-EBOV 疫苗接种 在怀孕期间的首次全面临床前安全性评估,并确定了妊娠早期脆弱性的关键窗口。更广泛地说,这项工作强调了干扰素在塑造胎盘 免疫应答 中的核心作用,并提供了一个实验框架来指导妊娠期活病毒 疫苗 的评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃博拉病毒病 是一种严重的病毒性出血性疾病,孕产妇和围产儿 死亡率 发病率较高。尽管基于水泡性口炎病毒的埃博拉 疫苗 (VSV-EBOV) 已在 暴发 环境中成功部署,但孕妇被排除在临床试验之外,从而留下了严重的安全漏洞。
设计与对象
为了解决这个问题,我们开发了一种小鼠模型来评估不同妊娠阶段暴露于 VSV-EBOV 后的妊娠结局。
核心方法
在胚胎第 7 天 (E7)、E10 或 E15 接种疫苗的怀孕 C57BL/6 小鼠产生了与非怀孕对照相当的强大抗 埃博拉病毒(抗 EBOV)抗体反应,并有效清除了 疫苗 病毒,而没有垂直 传播 的证据。
主要结果
然而,围植入期 疫苗接种 适度增加胎儿吸收和产后早期 死亡率,并伴有以血管重塑缺陷和炎症为特征的胎盘病理。
解释与创新
机制研究表明,这些不良反应是由 I 型干扰素信号传导而非直接病毒感染介导的,因为 IFN-β 缺陷小鼠表现出正常妊娠结局,并且尽管病毒载量较高,但阻断干扰素受体活性可改善胎盘病理。
科研与公卫意义
妊娠后期 疫苗接种 对生存或后代发育的影响很小。据我们所知,这些研究结果代表了 VSV-EBOV 疫苗接种 在怀孕期间的首次全面临床前安全性评估,并确定了妊娠早期脆弱性的关键窗口。
局限与证据强度
更广泛地说,这项工作强调了干扰素在塑造胎盘 免疫应答 中的核心作用,并提供了一个实验框架来指导妊娠期活病毒 疫苗 的评估。

Immunologic and placental responses to the VSV-EBOV vaccine in a pregnant mouse model.

Authors: Aaron P. Lewkowicz, Yi-Ru Wang, Mirian I Mendoza, Kaliroi Engel, Logan Kelley-Baker, Iara Rattner, Amber Lopez, Ian L. McWilliams, Derek Dc Ireland, Mohanraj Manangeeswaran
Original abstract (English)
Ebola virus disease is a severe viral hemorrhagic illness with high maternal and perinatal mortality. Although the replication-competent vesicular stomatitis virus-based Ebola vaccine (VSV-EBOV) has been deployed successfully in outbreak settings, pregnant women were excluded from clinical trials, leaving critical safety gaps. To address this, we developed a murine model to assess pregnancy outcomes following VSV-EBOV exposure at different gestational stages. Pregnant C57BL/6 mice vaccinated at embryonic day 7 (E7), E10, or E15 mounted robust anti-Ebola virus (anti-EBOV) antibody responses comparable to those of non-pregnant controls and efficiently cleared the vaccine virus without evidence of vertical transmission. However, peri-implantation vaccination modestly increased fetal resorption and early postnatal mortality, accompanied by placental pathology characterized by vascular remodeling defects and inflammation. Later gestational vaccination had minimal impact on survival or offspring development. Mechanistic studies revealed that these adverse effects were mediated by type I interferon signaling rather than direct viral infection, as IFN-β-deficient mice exhibited normal pregnancy outcomes and blocking interferon receptor activity ameliorated placental pathology despite higher viral loads. To our knowledge, these findings represent the first comprehensive preclinical safety evaluation of VSV-EBOV vaccination during pregnancy and identify a critical window of vulnerability early in gestation. More broadly, this work underscores the central role of interferons in shaping placental immune responses and provides an experimental framework to guide evaluation of live-virus vaccines in pregnancy.

抗病毒药物 治疗结果图集:病毒生物学如何影响 治疗 的影响范围

An Atlas of Antiviral Therapeutic Outcomes: How Viral Biology Shapes the Reach of Treatment
作者： Jithesh Othayoth, Jayant Singh Bist, Pradeep Bist
摘要中文翻译抗病毒药物 抑制不会产生单一的治疗终点:一些感染可以在有限的 治疗 后治愈,另一些感染需要持久抑制,而另一些感染仍然难以影响,因为可操作阶段短暂或在解剖学上难以接近。这些不同的结果反映了保持感染或允许复发的生物学状态、药物敏感过程仍然具有因果重要性的时间、相应病毒或病毒物质驻留的解剖空间以及病毒群体在治疗压力下进化的能力。在这里,我们研究了可治愈、可抑制、潜伏、急性、神经侵袭性和急性后病毒感染之间的关系。HCV、HIV-1、HBV、HSV、流感、RSV、登革热、SARS-CoV-2、埃博拉病毒、CHIKV 和其他系统之间的比较显示持久性架构、诊断时机、组织药理学、宿主 背景和耐药性如何决定 抗病毒药物 活性是否转化为病毒学治愈、持久抑制、复发控制、预防或不完全治疗控制。人类挑战研究、结果不一致的临床项目以及耐药性研究进一步说明了机械活性和治疗充分性之间的分歧。在这些例子中,抗病毒药物 的结果最终取决于 治疗 是否能够拦截决定感染后续命运的生物状态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索病毒生物学如何影响 抗病毒药物 治疗结果,强调 治疗 在不同病毒感染中成功的差异。
设计与对象
该研究检查了 埃博拉病毒病 在可治愈、可抑制、潜伏、急性、神经侵袭性和急性后病毒感染方面的结果,包括 埃博拉病毒病 和相关病症。
核心方法
作者对包括 埃博拉病毒 在内的各种病毒系统的 抗病毒药物 治疗结果进行了比较分析,以了解影响 治疗 疗效的因素。
主要结果
HCV、HIV-1、HBV、HSV、流感、RSV、登革热、SARS-CoV-2、埃博拉病毒、CHIKV 和其他系统之间的比较表明,持久性架构、诊断时机、组织药理学、宿主 背景和耐药性决定 抗病毒药物 结果。
解释与创新
作者解释说,抗病毒药物 结果取决于 治疗 是否能够拦截决定感染命运的生物状态,为理解治疗成功提供了一个新的框架。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果强调了病毒生物学在决定 治疗 成功和失败方面的重要性,为制定针对性的 抗病毒药物 策略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于抽象水平的比较,没有提供详细的实验数据,导致其结论的可信度中等。

An Atlas of Antiviral Therapeutic Outcomes: How Viral Biology Shapes the Reach of Treatment

Authors: Jithesh Othayoth, Jayant Singh Bist, Pradeep Bist
Original abstract (English)
Antiviral inhibition does not produce a single therapeutic endpoint: some infections can be cured after finite treatment, others require durable suppression, and others remain difficult to influence because the actionable phase is brief or anatomically inaccessible. These different outcomes reflect the biological state that preserves infection or permits return, the time during which a drug-sensitive process remains causally important, the anatomical space in which consequential virus or viral material resides, and the capacity of viral populations to evolve under therapeutic pressure. Here, we examine these relationships across curable, suppressible, latent, acute, neuroinvasive, and post-acute viral infections. Comparisons among HCV, HIV-1, HBV, HSV, influenza, RSV, dengue, SARS-CoV-2, Ebola virus, CHIKV, and other systems show how persistence architecture, diagnostic timing, tissue pharmacology, host context, and resistance can determine whether antiviral activity translates into virological cure, durable suppression, recurrence control, prevention, or incomplete therapeutic control. Human challenge studies, clinical programs with discordant outcomes, and resistance studies further illustrate where mechanistic activity and therapeutic sufficiency diverge. Across these examples, antiviral outcome depends ultimately on whether treatment can intercept the biological state that determines the subsequent fate of infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how viral biology influences antiviral therapeutic outcomes, highlighting the variability in treatment success across different viral infections.
Design and population
The study examined antiviral outcomes across curable, suppressible, latent, acute, neuroinvasive, and post-acute viral infections, including Ebola virus disease and related conditions.
Core methods
The authors conducted a comparative analysis of antiviral therapeutic outcomes across various viral systems, including Ebola virus, to understand the factors influencing treatment efficacy.
Main results
Comparisons among HCV, HIV-1, HBV, HSV, influenza, RSV, dengue, SARS-CoV-2, Ebola virus, CHIKV, and other systems revealed that persistence architecture, diagnostic timing, tissue pharmacology, host context, and resistance determine antiviral outcomes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that antiviral outcomes depend on whether treatment can intercept the biological state that dictates the fate of infection, offering a novel framework for understanding therapeutic success.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of targeted antiviral strategies by emphasizing the importance of viral biology in determining treatment success and failure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-level comparisons and does not provide detailed experimental data, resulting in moderate confidence in its conclusions.

超越长期新冠疫情:非洲急性感染后综合症的系统评价

Beyond long COVID: post-acute infection syndromes across Africa, a systematic review
作者： Sophie Müller, Riya Sharma, Katharina Heldt, Lynda Isaaka, Johanna Hanefeld, Ambrose Agweyu
摘要中文翻译急性感染后综合症(PAIS)通过长新冠病毒引起了全球关注,但许多与 PAIS 密切相关的病原体是非洲流行的,而那里的证据仍然匮乏;这一差距对卫生公平和全球卫生安全产生影响。我们的目的是系统地回顾非洲非新冠病毒感染后 PAIS 的 患病率、临床表现和相关因素,并确定未来研究的重点。我们进行了系统回顾,检索了截至 2025 年 11 月 7 日的 Medline、Embase 和灰色文献,寻找在非洲报告急性感染后四周或更长时间出现症状的研究。病例报告、案例系列、会议摘要、二次研究、评论和信件被排除在外。两名评审员独立筛选记录并提取有关研究特征、症状 患病率、临床表现和相关因素的数据。由于研究中观察到显着的异质性,因此对结果进行了描述性总结。该研究已在开放科学框架上注册。在 1,368 条记录中,纳入了 65 项研究,其中 42 项涉及 埃博拉病毒病,19 项涉及基孔肯雅热,具有中度至低度偏倚风险。埃博拉病毒后 PAIS 的 患病率 范围为 17.5% 至 98.6%,基孔肯雅热病毒后 PAIS 范围为 12.1% 至 78.6%。埃博拉病毒感染后主要表现为肌肉骨骼疼痛、眼部并发症、头痛和疲劳;关节痛是后基孔肯雅病的标志。严重的症状负担在急性感染后持续数年。女性、年龄较大和急性疾病严重程度是最常报告的与持续性后遗症相关的因素。一项探索性的、非系统性的补充搜索还发现了女性幸存者中特定性别的后果,包括月经不调和不良妊娠结局。尽管证据稀缺、异质且地理集中,但报告的幸存者中持续症状的 患病率 很高,尽管大多数研究中缺乏对照组使感染本身的归因变得复杂。非洲的卫生系统必须适应提供长期幸存者护理,将后续行动纳入现有的慢性病护理平台。由于非洲流行的病原体具有大流行的可能性并在地理上扩大,了解其长期后果既是卫生公平问题,也是全球卫生安全的紧迫优先事项。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性感染后综合症(PAIS)通过长新冠病毒引起了全球关注,但许多与 PAIS 密切相关的病原体是非洲流行的,而那里的证据仍然匮乏;这一差距对卫生公平和全球卫生安全产生影响。
设计与对象
我们进行了系统回顾,检索了截至 2025 年 11 月 7 日的 Medline、Embase 和灰色文献,寻找在非洲报告急性感染后四周或更长时间出现症状的研究。
核心方法
两名评审员独立筛选记录并提取有关研究特征、症状 患病率、临床表现和相关因素的数据。
主要结果
埃博拉病毒后 PAIS 的 患病率 范围为 17.5% 至 98.6%,基孔肯雅热病毒后 PAIS 范围为 12.1% 至 78.6%。
解释与创新
尽管证据稀缺、异质且地理集中,但报告的幸存者中持续症状的 患病率 很高,尽管大多数研究中缺乏对照组使感染本身的归因变得复杂。
科研与公卫意义
非洲的卫生系统必须适应提供长期幸存者护理,将后续行动纳入现有的慢性病护理平台。
局限与证据强度
由于研究中观察到显着的异质性,因此对结果进行了描述性总结。

Beyond long COVID: post-acute infection syndromes across Africa, a systematic review

Authors: Sophie Müller, Riya Sharma, Katharina Heldt, Lynda Isaaka, Johanna Hanefeld, Ambrose Agweyu
Original abstract (English)
Post-acute infection syndromes (PAIS) have gained global attention through Long COVID, yet many pathogens most closely linked to PAIS are endemic to Africa, where evidence remains scarce; a gap with implications for health equity and global health security. We aimed to systematically review the prevalence, clinical presentation, and associated factors of PAIS following non-COVID infections in Africa, and to identify priorities for future research. We conducted a systematic review, searching Medline, Embase, and grey literature until 7 November 2025 for studies in Africa reporting symptoms four or more weeks after acute infection. Case reports, case series, conference abstracts, secondary research, comments, and correspondence were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened records and extracted data on study characteristics, symptom prevalence, clinical presentation, and associated factors. As substantial heterogeneity was observed across studies, results were summarised descriptively. The study was registered on the Open Science Framework. Of 1,368 records, 65 studies were included, 42 on Ebola virus disease and 19 on chikungunya, with moderate to low risk of bias. Prevalence of PAIS ranged from 17.5% to 98.6% after Ebola and 12.1% to 78.6% after chikungunya. Musculoskeletal pain, ocular complications, headache, and fatigue predominated after Ebola; arthralgia was the hallmark of post-chikungunya disease. Substantial symptom burden persisted years after acute infection. Female sex, older age, and greater acute disease severity were the factors most frequently reported to be associated with persistent sequelae. An exploratory, non-systematic supplementary search additionally identified sex-specific consequences among female survivors including menstrual irregularities and adverse pregnancy outcomes. Despite scarce, heterogeneous, and geographically concentrated evidence, the reported prevalence of persistent symptoms among survivors is high, although the absence of comparison groups in most studies complicates attribution to the infection itself. Health systems in Africa must adapt to provide long-term survivor care, embedding follow-up into existing chronic care platforms. As pathogens endemic to Africa carry pandemic potential and expand geographically, understanding their chronic consequences is both a matter of health equity and an urgent global health security priority.
View research seven-element analysis

暴发 环境中的本迪布焦病毒病:实用临床回顾

Bundibugyo virus disease in outbreak settings: A practical clinical review
作者： Samriddhi Srivastava, Ananya Kaul, Mudit Singla, Tweheyo Ronald
摘要中文翻译本迪布焦病毒病 (BVD) 是由本迪布焦病毒 (BDBV) 引起的严重丝状病毒感染,BDBV 是一种正埃博拉病毒,具有与 埃博拉病毒 (EBOV) 不同的诊断和对策意义。截至 2026 年 9 月 23 日,刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 正在发生的 暴发 疫情已大幅扩大,七个省的 63 个卫生区报告了 7890 例确诊病例和 3799 例死亡。BVD 通常表现为一种非特异性发热和胃肠道疾病;就诊时出血表现并不常见。早期的 暴发 调查还表明,以 EBOV 为导向的检测可能无法检测到 BDBV,因此当 BDBV 流行病学上合理时,适合物种的分子检测至关重要。支持性护理仍然是管理标准,但外围设施和专门的丝状病毒 治疗 中心之间可以安全提供的护理有所不同。首次接触设施应优先考虑识别、分离、受保护采样、立即危及生命或常见可治疗病症的 治疗 以及早期转诊;治疗 中心可以提供更密切的监测、先进的支持护理以及获得批准的研究方案。不安全、流离失所、实验室和运输限制以及提交延迟继续限制实施。尚无 疫苗 或特定治疗方法已确定针对人类 BDBV 的临床疗效;Ervebo 正在研究方案中进行评估,PARTNERS 试验正在评估 MBP134 和基于瑞德西韦的治疗方案。本综述综合了关于受 诊断 影响的环境中的识别、诊断、临床护理、感染预防、转诊和新兴对策的历史和当前证据。

证据基础与方法
提供的证据未明确说明综述方法、数据库、筛选过程或证据基础。
共识与结论
早期的 暴发 调查还表明,以 EBOV 为导向的检测可能无法检测到 BDBV,因此当 BDBV 流行病学上合理时,适合物种的分子检测至关重要。
争议与缺口
不安全、流离失所、实验室和运输限制以及提交延迟继续限制实施。
科研与实践启示
本综述综合了有关受 诊断 影响的环境中的识别、诊断、临床护理、感染预防、转诊和新兴对策的历史和当前证据。

Bundibugyo virus disease in outbreak settings: A practical clinical review

Authors: Samriddhi Srivastava, Ananya Kaul, Mudit Singla, Tweheyo Ronald
Original abstract (English)
Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) is a severe filovirus infection caused by Bundibugyo virus (BDBV), an orthoebolavirus with diagnostic and countermeasure implications distinct from Ebola virus (EBOV). The ongoing outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) has expanded substantially, with 7890 confirmed cases and 3799 deaths reported across 63 health zones in seven provinces as of 23 September 2026. BVD usually presents as a nonspecific febrile and gastrointestinal illness; haemorrhagic manifestations are uncommon at presentation. Early outbreak investigation also showed that EBOV-oriented testing can fail to detect BDBV, making species-appropriate molecular testing essential when BDBV is epidemiologically plausible. Supportive care remains the standard of management, but what can be delivered safely differs between peripheral facilities and dedicated filovirus treatment centres. First-contact facilities should prioritise recognition, separation, protected sampling, treatment of immediately life-threatening or common treatable conditions, and early referral; treatment centres can provide closer monitoring, advanced supportive care, and access to approved research protocols. Insecurity, displacement, laboratory and transport constraints, and delayed presentation continue to limit implementation. No vaccine or specific therapeutic has established clinical efficacy against BDBV in humans; Ervebo is being evaluated within research protocols and the PARTNERS trial is evaluating MBP134 and remdesivir-based regimens. This review synthesises historical and current evidence on recognition, diagnosis, clinical care, infection prevention, referral, and emerging countermeasures in outbreak-affected settings.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
No vaccine or specific therapeutic has established clinical efficacy against BDBV in humans; Ervebo is being evaluated within research protocols and the PARTNERS trial is evaluating MBP134 and remdesivir-based regimens.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
Early outbreak investigation also showed that EBOV-oriented testing can fail to detect BDBV, making species-appropriate molecular testing essential when BDBV is epidemiologically plausible.
Controversies and gaps
Insecurity, displacement, laboratory and transport constraints, and delayed presentation continue to limit implementation.
Research and practice implications
This review synthesises historical and current evidence on recognition, diagnosis, clinical care, infection prevention, referral, and emerging countermeasures in outbreak-affected settings.

尼日利亚本迪布焦病毒病和军事卫生设施:对临床、诊断和对策证据以及 监测 整合差距的快速审查。

Bundibugyo virus disease and military health facilities in Nigeria: a rapid review of clinical, diagnostic and countermeasure evidence and surveillance integration gaps.
作者： Abba Inalegwu Owoicho, Chukwuemeka Kennedy Ikechukwu, Kateanah Ibukunoluwa Akinmeji, James Ismailu Fatika, Patrick Tobechi Ashinze, Basila Inie Allagoa, Bala Musa Halilu
摘要中文翻译该项目对本迪布焦病毒病 (BVD) 的证据及其对尼日利亚军事卫生设施的影响进行了快速审查。本迪布焦病毒(BDBV)在刚果民主共和国造成了有记录以来最大规模的埃博拉疫情,并于 2026 年 5 月宣布为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件,乌干达、肯尼亚和欧洲出现了病例,并于 2026 年 10 月 20 日在尼日利亚阿布贾出现了输入性病例。整个非洲的埃博拉病毒防范工作是围绕另一种物种扎伊尔埃博拉病毒展开的:获得许可的 疫苗、获得许可的单克隆抗体疗法和最广泛部署的患者附近诊断检测均以该物种为目标,但没有一个被批准用于 BDBV。审查有两个目的。第一个是整合截至 2026 年 10 月下旬有关 BDBV 病毒学、临床特征、诊断、治疗、疫苗 和感染预防的现有证据。第二个是检查尼日利亚军事卫生设施如何与国家综合疾病 监测 和响应 (IDSR) 系统连接。这些设施为国际部署人员、他们的家人以及通常的平民患者提供服务,但通过单独的指挥链进行报告,并且没有已发表的研究衡量他们的 监测 报告。方法遵循 Cochrane 快速评价方法组指南,使用 PRISMA 2020 和 PRISMA-S 报告。PubMed、Europe PMC、HAL、African Journals Online 和 ClinicalTrials.gov 的检索于 2026 年 10 月 24 日进行,并由 WHO、非洲 CDC、ECDC、美国 CDC、尼日利亚政府和联合国来源进行补充。筛选由一位审稿人进行,并由第二位审稿人验证,综合是叙述性的。预期成果是为西非临床医生和公共卫生人员提供 BDBV 证据的最新综合,描述军事卫生设施相对于尼日利亚国家 监测 的位置,以及这些设施的一系列准备优先事项,包括 监测 连接、返回人员筛查、病例定义、实验室路径、隔离准备、联合接触者追踪和试验准入。进一步的预期结果是记录证据差距:初步搜索发现没有研究衡量尼日利亚军事设施的 IDSR 报告。如果得到证实,缺席本身就是一个发现,审查将提出设施级评估作为下一次研究的优先事项。检索策略、记录列表、筛选决策和文献矩阵将随本项目一起存放。该方案在检索和第一次标题和摘要筛选之后、但在全文筛选、数据提取和合成之前注册;未前瞻性地注册任何协议。

共识与结论
筛选由一位审稿人进行,并由第二位审稿人验证,综合是叙述性的。

科研与实践启示
预期成果是为西非临床医生和公共卫生人员提供 BDBV 证据的最新综合,描述军事卫生设施相对于尼日利亚国家 监测 的位置,以及这些设施的一系列准备优先事项,包括 监测 连接、返回人员筛查、病例定义、实验室路径、隔离准备、联合接触者追踪和试验准入。

Bundibugyo virus disease and military health facilities in Nigeria: a rapid review of clinical, diagnostic and countermeasure evidence and surveillance integration gaps.

Authors: Abba Inalegwu Owoicho, Chukwuemeka Kennedy Ikechukwu, Kateanah Ibukunoluwa Akinmeji, James Ismailu Fatika, Patrick Tobechi Ashinze, Basila Inie Allagoa, Bala Musa Halilu
Original abstract (English)
This project is a rapid review of the evidence on Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) and its implications for military health facilities in Nigeria. Bundibugyo virus (BDBV) caused the largest Ebola disease epidemic ever recorded in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, declared a Public Health Emergency of International Concern in May 2026, with onward cases in Uganda, Kenya, Europe and, on 20 October 2026, an imported case in Abuja, Nigeria. Ebola preparedness across Africa was built around a different species, Zaire ebolavirus: the licensed vaccine, the licensed monoclonal antibody therapies and the most widely deployed near-patient diagnostic assay all target that species, and none is approved for BDBV. The review has two purposes. The first is to consolidate current evidence on BDBV virology, clinical features, diagnosis, treatment, vaccines and infection prevention, as of late October 2026. The second is to examine how Nigerian military health facilities connect to the national Integrated Disease Surveillance and Response (IDSR) system. These facilities serve internationally deployed personnel, their families and often civilian patients, but report through a separate command chain, and no published study has measured their surveillance reporting. Methods follow Cochrane Rapid Reviews Methods Group guidance, reported using PRISMA 2020 and PRISMA-S. Searches of PubMed, Europe PMC, HAL, African Journals Online and ClinicalTrials.gov were run on 24 October 2026, supplemented by WHO, Africa CDC, ECDC, US CDC, Nigerian government and United Nations sources. Screening is by one reviewer with second-reviewer verification, and synthesis is narrative. Expected outcomes are a current synthesis of BDBV evidence for clinicians and public health staff in West Africa, a description of how military health facilities sit relative to national surveillance in Nigeria, and a set of preparedness priorities for those facilities covering surveillance connection, screening of returning personnel, case definitions, laboratory pathways, isolation readiness, joint contact tracing and trial access. A further expected outcome is documentation of an evidence gap: preliminary searching found no study measuring IDSR reporting from Nigerian military facilities. If confirmed, that absence is itself a finding, and the review will propose a facility-level assessment as the priority next study. The search strategy, record list, screening decisions and literature matrix will be deposited with this project. The protocol was registered after the searches and a first pass of title and abstract screening, but before full-text screening, data extraction and synthesis; no protocol was registered prospectively.
View review five-element analysis

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions

SARS、H1N1、埃博拉、COVID-19、MPOX、H5N1 和汉坦病毒:人类正在进入流行病的新时代吗?土耳其准备好迎接下一个全球 暴发 了吗?

SARS, H1N1, EBOLA, COVID-19, MPOX, H5N1 AND HANTAVIRUS: IS HUMANITY ENTERING A NEW ERA OF PANDEMIC? IS TÜRKİYE PREPARED FOR THE NEXT GLOBAL OUTBREAK?
作者： Sebla Ak
摘要中文翻译传染病的出现和再次出现对全球卫生安全构成持续挑战。暴发,例如严重急性呼吸系统综合症 (SARS)、甲型流感 (H1N1)、埃博拉病毒病、2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19)、mpox、甲型禽流感 (H5N1) 和汉坦病毒感染,凸显了针对新出现的传染病威胁做好有效准备的必要性。“X疾病”的概念进一步强调了为可能导致未来流行病或大流行的未知病原体做好准备的重要性。本研究旨在检查主要传染病 暴发 并评估土耳其对未来潜在的全球 暴发 的准备情况。基于对科学出版物和机构报告的结构化文献检索和文档分析,采用了描述性综述设计。使用 SWOT 框架对调查结果进行组织和解释。该分析将医疗基础设施、重症监护能力、数字医疗系统以及在 COVID-19 大流行期间获得的经验确定为潜在优势。然而,与医疗保健劳动力能力、人畜共患疾病、公共卫生素养、人类、动物和环境卫生部门之间的协调以及某些生物技术产品对外部来源的依赖相关的挑战可能会限制准备工作。气候变化、环境破坏、国际旅行、移民和全球贸易被认为是可能影响传染病出现和传播的因素。加强综合 监测 和早期预警系统,通过“同一个健康”方法改善部门间协调,提高国内 疫苗 和诊断生产能力,以及扩大基于社区的公共卫生教育,可能有助于提高土耳其对未来 暴发 的准备。这些发现凸显了针对新出现的传染病威胁进行持续、多部门准备的重要性。

证据基础与方法
基于对科学出版物和机构报告的结构化文献检索和文档分析,采用了描述性综述设计。使用 SWOT 框架对调查结果进行组织和解释。
共识与结论
该分析将医疗基础设施、重症监护能力、数字医疗系统以及在 COVID-19 大流行期间获得的经验确定为潜在优势。暴发,例如严重急性呼吸系统综合症 (SARS)、甲型流感 (H1N1)、埃博拉病毒病、2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19)、mpox、甲型禽流感 (H5N1) 和汉坦病毒感染,凸显了针对新出现的传染病威胁做好有效准备的必要性。
争议与缺口
与医疗保健劳动力能力、人畜共患疾病 监测、公共卫生素养、人类、动物和环境卫生部门之间的协调以及某些生物技术产品对外部来源的依赖相关的挑战可能会限制准备工作。气候变化、环境破坏、国际旅行、移民和全球贸易被认为是可能影响传染病出现和传播的因素。
科研与实践启示
加强综合 监测 和早期预警系统,通过“同一个健康”方法改善部门间协调,提高国内 疫苗 和诊断生产能力,以及扩大基于社区的公共卫生教育,可能有助于提高土耳其对未来 暴发 的准备。这些发现凸显了针对新出现的传染病威胁进行持续、多部门准备的重要性。

SARS, H1N1, EBOLA, COVID-19, MPOX, H5N1 AND HANTAVIRUS: IS HUMANITY ENTERING A NEW ERA OF PANDEMIC? IS TÜRKİYE PREPARED FOR THE NEXT GLOBAL OUTBREAK?

Authors: Sebla Ak
Original abstract (English)
The emergence and re-emergence of infectious diseases pose persistent challenges to global health security. Outbreaks such as severe acute respiratory syndrome (SARS), influenza A(H1N1), Ebola virus disease, coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19), mpox, avian influenza A(H5N1), and hantavirus infections highlight the need for effective preparedness against emerging infectious threats. The concept of “Disease X” further emphasizes the importance of preparing for unknown pathogens that may cause future epidemics or pandemics. This study aimed to examine major infectious disease outbreaks and assess Türkiye’s preparedness for potential future global outbreaks. A descriptive review design was employed, based on a structured literature search and document analysis of scientific publications and institutional reports. The findings were organized and interpreted using a SWOT framework. The analysis identified healthcare infrastructure, intensive care capacity, digital health systems, and experience gained during the COVID-19 pandemic as potential strengths. However, challenges related to healthcare workforce capacity, zoonotic disease surveillance, public health literacy, coordination across human, animal, and environmental health sectors, and dependence on external sources for certain biotechnological products may constrain preparedness. Climate change, environmental disruption, international travel, migration, and global trade were identified as factors that may influence the emergence and spread of infectious diseases. Strengthening integrated surveillance and early warning systems, improving intersectoral coordination through the One Health approach, enhancing domestic vaccine and diagnostic production capacity, and expanding community-based public health education may help improve Türkiye’s preparedness for future outbreaks. These findings highlight the importance of sustained, multisectoral preparedness for emerging infectious disease threats.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The emergence and re-emergence of infectious diseases pose persistent challenges to global health security. This study aimed to examine major infectious disease outbreaks and assess Türkiye’s preparedness for potential future global outbreaks.
Evidence base and method
A descriptive review design was employed, based on a structured literature search and document analysis of scientific publications and institutional reports. The findings were organized and interpreted using a SWOT framework.
Consensus and conclusions
The analysis identified healthcare infrastructure, intensive care capacity, digital health systems, and experience gained during the COVID-19 pandemic as potential strengths. Outbreaks such as severe acute respiratory syndrome (SARS), influenza A(H1N1), Ebola virus disease, coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19), mpox, avian influenza A(H5N1), and hantavirus infections highlight the need for effective preparedness against emerging infectious threats.
Controversies and gaps
Challenges related to healthcare workforce capacity, zoonotic disease surveillance, public health literacy, coordination across human, animal, and environmental health sectors, and dependence on external sources for certain biotechnological products may constrain preparedness. Climate change, environmental disruption, international travel, migration, and global trade were identified as factors that may influence the emergence and spread of infectious diseases.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening integrated surveillance and early warning systems, improving intersectoral coordination through the One Health approach, enhancing domestic vaccine and diagnostic production capacity, and expanding community-based public health education may help improve Türkiye’s preparedness for future outbreaks. These findings highlight the importance of sustained, multisectoral preparedness for emerging infectious disease threats.

人类RNA病毒对肌动蛋白的重塑:机制和靶向干预

Actin remodeling by human RNA virus: mechanisms and targeted intervention
作者： Jie Gao, Wei Yu
摘要中文翻译肌动蛋白细胞骨架是真核细胞中一个核心的动态网络,负责维持细胞形态、运动性和细胞内运输。大量研究表明,肌动蛋白在RNA病毒感染中具有重要作用。肌动蛋白重塑作为RNA病毒利用的核心宿主机制,强烈影响病毒复制和传播效率。本文综述了参与肌动蛋白动态调控的关键调节分子。在此基础上,我们重点探讨了六种代表性的人类RNA病毒(严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒-2、甲型流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒、登革病毒、埃博拉病毒和人类免疫缺陷病毒)如何劫持宿主肌动蛋白网络,揭示RNA病毒倾向于选择的肌动蛋白劫持途径。最后,我们进一步总结了当前针对抗病毒药物化合物作用于肌动蛋白信号通路的研究现状。鉴于针对宿主因子具有独特的广谱抗病毒药物活性优势,这种策略为克服高病毒突变率导致的耐药性提供了希望。然而,其临床应用仍面临平衡细胞毒性与疗效的困难。本文旨在为针对肌动蛋白的新型抗病毒药物干预方法提供概念框架。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
肌动蛋白细胞骨架是真核细胞中的中央动态网络,它保留形态、运动性和细胞内运输。广泛的研究已经证实肌动蛋白在RNA病毒感染中的重要性。肌动蛋白重塑作为RNA病毒利用的核心宿主机制,强烈影响病毒复制和传播效率。本文综述了肌动蛋白动力学中涉及的关键调控分子,重点介绍了六种代表性人类RNA病毒劫持宿主肌动蛋白网络的分子机制的最新进展,揭示了RNA病毒倾向于选择的肌动蛋白劫持途径。最后,文章总结了针对肌动蛋白信号通路的抗病毒药物化合物的研究现状。
证据基础与方法
所提供的证据中未明确报告综述类型、数据库、检索日期、资格标准、选择方法、纳入研究计数以及荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
靶向 宿主 因子提供了广谱 抗病毒药物 活性的独特优势,这为克服高病毒 突变 率引起的耐药性提供了希望。
争议与缺口
鉴于针对 宿主 因子在平衡细胞毒性和功效方面仍然面临困难,这代表了具体的证据差距。靶向肌动蛋白信号通路进行抗病毒药物干预的临床应用仍面临挑战。
科研与实践启示
根据综述,研究、监测、诊断、治疗和政策的具体优先事项包括进一步研究RNA病毒劫持肌动蛋白的分子机制以及开发针对肌动蛋白信号通路的抗病毒药物化合物。该策略为克服高病毒突变率引起的耐药性提供了希望,但临床应用仍面临平衡细胞毒性和疗效的困难。

Actin remodeling by human RNA virus: mechanisms and targeted intervention

Authors: Jie Gao, Wei Yu
Original abstract (English)
The actin cytoskeleton is a central dynamic network in eukaryotic cells which preserves morphology, motility, and intracellular transport. Extensive research has confirmed the importance of actin in RNA viral infection. Actin remodeling, as a core host mechanism exploited by RNA viruses, strongly affects viral replication and transmission efficiency. This article reviews the key regulatory molecules involved in actin dynamics. Based on this foundation, we focus on recent advances in the molecular mechanisms by which six representative human RNA viruses(severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus-2, influenza A virus, respiratory syncytial virus, dengue virus, ebola virus and human immunodeficiency virus) hijack the host actin network, revealing the actin hijacking pathways that RNA viruses tend to select. Finally, we further summarize the current status of research on antiviral compounds that target the actin signaling pathway. Given that targeting host factors offers the unique advantage of broad-spectrum antiviral activity, this strategy offers promise for overcoming drug resistance caused by high viral mutation rates. However, its clinical application still faces difficulties in balancing cytotoxicity and efficacy. This article intends to provide a conceptual framework for novel antiviral intervention approaches targeting actin.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The actin cytoskeleton is a central dynamic network in eukaryotic cells which preserves morphology, motility, and intracellular transport. Extensive research has confirmed the importance of actin in RNA viral infection. Actin remodeling, as a core host mechanism exploited by RNA viruses, strongly affects viral replication and transmission efficiency. This article reviews the key regulatory molecules involved in actin dynamics and focuses on recent advances in the molecular mechanisms by which six representative human RNA viruses hijack the host actin network, revealing the actin hijacking pathways that RNA viruses tend to select. Finally, the article summarizes the current status of research on antiviral compounds that target the actin signaling pathway.
Evidence base and method
The review type, databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, selection method, included-study count, and meta-analysis or consensus process are not explicitly reported in the provided evidence.
Consensus and conclusions
Targeting host factors offers the unique advantage of broad-spectrum antiviral activity, which offers promise for overcoming drug resistance caused by high viral mutation rates.
Controversies and gaps
Given that targeting host factors still faces difficulties in balancing cytotoxicity and efficacy, this represents a concrete evidence gap. The clinical application of targeting actin signaling pathways for antiviral intervention still faces challenges.
Research and practice implications
Based on the review, specific priorities for research, surveillance, diagnosis, treatment, and policy include further investigation into the molecular mechanisms of actin hijacking by RNA viruses and the development of antiviral compounds that target the actin signaling pathway. This strategy offers promise for overcoming drug resistance caused by high viral mutation rates, but clinical application still faces difficulties in balancing cytotoxicity and efficacy.

严重后果传染病患者的生物防护运输:紧急医疗系统的叙述回顾和综合运营框架

Biocontainment Transport of Patients with High-Consequence Infectious Diseases: A Narrative Review and Integrated Operational Framework for Emergency Medical Systems
作者： D'Urso F, Mara Gracy Basile, Edoardo Falcone, Ulrico Angeloni, Federico D’Urso
摘要中文翻译背景:/目标:严重后果传染病(HCID)患者的转运将生物安全科学和急诊医学联系在一起。2026年的本迪布焦病毒病疫情以及与一艘探险游轮相关的多国安第斯山脉病毒暴发再次引起人们对如何在社区、医院和高级隔离单位之间安全转移此类患者的关注。这篇叙述性综述综合了有关生物防护运输的国际文献,并将其整合到紧急医疗系统的操作框架中。方法:根据 SANRA 量表,对 PubMed/MEDLINE 和 Scopus(截至 2026 年 10 月 10 日)进行结构化检索,并辅以世界卫生组织、欧洲疾病预防和控制中心、美国疾病控制和预防中心以及国家当局的指导。证据跨七个业务领域进行组织,并结合意大利红十字会生物防护运输服务的运营经验。结果:在已识别的 2432 条记录中,筛选出 1485 条独特记录。文献以航空医学病例报告、技术评估和专家共识为主,比较研究较少。反复出现的调查结果涉及运输隔离器内的设备完整性和空气质量、落纱过程中的自我污染风险、个人防护设备造成的热应变和通信限制、车辆和隔离器的净化、低活动期间团队准备状态的保持以及患者和工作人员的心理负担。这些发现被整合到一个框架中,该框架包括四链接遏制链、七阶段操作工作流程、三层复杂性分类和基于角色的责任矩阵。结论:生物防护运输最好理解为一个综合应急系统,而不是一种以设备为中心的干预措施。拟议的框架是一个结构化的、基于共识的规划、培训和质量保证参考;其组成部分需要前瞻性评估。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查的重点是严重后果传染病(HCID)患者的运输,特别是检查生物防护实践。核心问题是紧急医疗系统如何安全地转移感染人类致病性正埃博拉病毒及相关病毒的患者,重点是操作框架和系统集成。
证据基础与方法
该综述在世界卫生组织、欧洲疾病预防和控制中心、美国疾病控制和预防中心以及国家当局的指导下,对截至 2026 年 10 月 10 日的 PubMed/MEDLINE 和 Scopus 进行了结构化检索。研究结果根据 SANRA 等级进行报告,并结合意大利红十字会生物防护运输服务的运营经验。
共识与结论
文献以航空医学病例报告、技术评估、专家共识为主,比较研究较少。反复出现的调查结果包括运输隔离器内的设备完整性和空气质量、落纱过程中的自我污染风险、热应变、个人防护设备造成的通信限制、车辆和隔离器的净化、低活动期间的团队准备情况以及患者和工作人员的心理负担。这些发现被整合到一个框架中,该框架包括四链接遏制链、七阶段操作工作流程、三层复杂性分类和基于角色的责任矩阵。
争议与缺口
文献缺乏比较研究,且以病例报告和专家共识为主,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。还缺乏对框架组成部分的前瞻性评估,审查指出,意大利红十字会生物防护运输服务的运营经验被用来补充证据。
科研与实践启示
拟议的框架是一个结构化的、基于共识的规划、培训和质量保证参考。该框架的组成部分需要进行前瞻性评估以验证其有效性。此外,该审查强调需要进一步研究比较研究和生物防护运输的操作挑战。

Biocontainment Transport of Patients with High-Consequence Infectious Diseases: A Narrative Review and Integrated Operational Framework for Emergency Medical Systems

Authors: D'Urso F, Mara Gracy Basile, Edoardo Falcone, Ulrico Angeloni, Federico D’Urso
Original abstract (English)
Background: /Objectives: The transport of patients with high-consequence infectious diseases (HCIDs) links biosafety science and emergency medicine. The 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease epidemic and the multinational Andes virus outbreak associated with an expedition cruise ship have renewed attention on how such patients are moved safely between the community, hospitals and high-level isolation units. This narrative review synthesises the international literature on biocontainment transport and integrates it into an operational framework for emergency medical systems. Methods: A structured search of PubMed/MEDLINE and Scopus (to 10 October 2026), complemented by guidance from the World Health Organization, the European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control, the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention and national authorities, was conducted and reported according to the SANRA scale. Evidence was organised across seven operational domains and combined with the operational experience of the Italian Red Cross biocontainment transport service. Results: Of 2432 records identified, 1485 unique records were screened. The literature is dominated by aeromedical case reports, technical evaluations and expert consensus, with few comparative studies. Recurrent findings concern device integrity and air quality within transport isolators, the risk of self-contamination during doffing, heat strain and communication limits imposed by personal protective equipment, decontamination of vehicles and isolators, the maintenance of team readiness during periods of low activity, and the psychological burden on patients and staff. These findings are integrated into a framework comprising a four-link containment chain, a seven-phase operational workflow, a three-tier complexity classification and a role-based responsibility matrix. Conclusions: Biocontainment transport is better understood as an integrated emergency system than as a device-centred intervention. The proposed framework is a structured, consensus-based reference for planning, training and quality assurance; its components require prospective evaluation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the transport of patients with high-consequence infectious diseases (HCIDs), specifically examining biocontainment practices. The central question is how emergency medical systems can safely move patients infected with human-pathogenic orthoebolaviruses and related viruses, with an emphasis on operational frameworks and system integration.
Evidence base and method
The review conducted a structured search of PubMed/MEDLINE and Scopus up to 10 October 2026, guided by the World Health Organization, the European Centre for Disease Prevention and Control, the US Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, and national authorities. The findings were reported according to the SANRA scale and integrated with the operational experience of the Italian Red Cross biocontainment transport service.
Consensus and conclusions
The literature is dominated by aeromedical case reports, technical evaluations, and expert consensus, with few comparative studies. Recurrent findings include device integrity and air quality within transport isolators, the risk of self-contamination during doffing, heat strain, communication limits imposed by personal protective equipment, decontamination of vehicles and isolators, team readiness during low-activity periods, and the psychological burden on patients and staff. These findings are integrated into a framework comprising a four-link containment chain, a seven-phase operational workflow, a three-tier complexity classification, and a role-based responsibility matrix.
Controversies and gaps
The literature lacks comparative studies and is dominated by case reports and expert consensus, which may limit the generalizability of findings. There is also a lack of prospective evaluation of the framework components, and the review notes that the operational experience of the Italian Red Cross biocontainment transport service was used to complement the evidence.
Research and practice implications
The proposed framework is a structured, consensus-based reference for planning, training, and quality assurance. The components of the framework require prospective evaluation to validate their effectiveness. Additionally, the review highlights the need for further research on comparative studies and the operational challenges of biocontainment transport.

Published: 2026-10-27  |  Publisher: ReliefWeb
A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

联合国儿童基金会刚果民主共和国人道主义局势第 4 号报告——埃博拉 暴发(本迪布焦病毒),9 月 30 日 - ReliefWeb

UNICEF Democratic Republic of the Congo Humanitarian Situation Report No. 4 – Ebola Outbreak (Bundibugyo Virus), 30 September - ReliefWeb
新闻简报The UNICEF Humanitarian Situation Report No. 4 from the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) highlights the ongoing Bundibugyo virus outbreak, which has expanded to seven provinces, including Sud-Ubangi. As of 30 September 2026, the DRC reported 2,050 additional confirmed cases and 979 deaths, bringing cumulative confirmed cases to 8,300 and deaths to 4,018, with a case fatality ratio of 48.4 per cent. Children under five account for 63.8 per cent of deaths among children aged 17 and below, and children aged 17 years and below represent 27.4 per cent of confirmed cases and 37.6 per cent of deaths. Women and girls make up 53.4 per cent of confirmed cases. The outbreak is the largest in the DRC and the second largest globally. UNICEF has scaled up operations to 63 health zones, maintaining a presence in all 16 hotspots. The organization has deployed 3,469 additional community mobilizers and expanded its response to include more than 590,000 litres of safe water, support to health facilities and schools, and decontamination capacity. UNICEF requires US$ 113.5 million for its revised six-month response plan, with US$ 41 million mobilized as of 30 September, leaving a US$ 72.5 million funding gap. Timely, flexible funding is urgently needed to sustain outbreak control and protect vulnerable populations, including children, in areas affected by conflict and displacement.
查看新闻五要素
时间
截至2026年9月30日,暴发的地域范围继续扩大,南乌班吉省首次报告确诊病例,使受影响的省份数量达到7个。9 月份,又有 3 个卫生区报告确诊病例,受影响卫生区总数增至 63 个。该报告的发布日期为2026年10月27日。
地点与对象
The Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), specifically in Ituri, North Kivu, South Kivu, Haut-Uélé, Tshopo, Bas-Uélé, and Sud-Ubangi provinces. Children under five bear the greatest burden, representing approximately 63.8 per cent of deaths among children aged 17 years and below. Women and girls represent approximately 53.4 per cent of reported confirmed cases.
事件
由本迪布焦病毒引起的埃博拉病毒 暴发,刚果民主共和国报告了确诊病例和死亡病例。暴发 目前是刚果民主共和国有史以来最大的、全球第二大的。联合国儿童基金会将其多部门行动从 60 个卫生区扩大到 63 个,在所有 16 个热点地区保持存在。
规模、影响与风险
Between 1 and 30 September 2026, the DRC reported 2,050 additional confirmed Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) cases and 979 deaths. As of 30 September, cumulative confirmed cases had reached 8,300, including 4,018 deaths, corresponding to a case fatality ratio (CFR) of 48.4 per cent. Children aged 17 years and below account for approximately 27.4 per cent of confirmed cases and 37.6 per cent of reported deaths, indicating a disproportionate mortality burden among children.
应对、状态与不确定性
Community-based surveillance was strengthened through the deployment of 3,469 additional community mobilizers. UNICEF requires US$ 113.5 million for its revised six-month response plan, with US$ 41 million mobilized as of 30 September, leaving a US$ 72.5 million funding gap.

UNICEF Democratic Republic of the Congo Humanitarian Situation Report No. 4 – Ebola Outbreak (Bundibugyo Virus), 30 September - ReliefWeb

News Brief
The UNICEF Humanitarian Situation Report No. 4 from the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) highlights the ongoing Bundibugyo virus outbreak, which has expanded to seven provinces, including Sud-Ubangi. As of 30 September 2026, the DRC reported 2,050 additional confirmed cases and 979 deaths, bringing cumulative confirmed cases to 8,300 and deaths to 4,018, with a case fatality ratio of 48.4 per cent. Children under five account for 63.8 per cent of deaths among children aged 17 and below, and children aged 17 years and below represent 27.4 per cent of confirmed cases and 37.6 per cent of deaths. Women and girls make up 53.4 per cent of confirmed cases. The outbreak is the largest in the DRC and the second largest globally. UNICEF has scaled up operations to 63 health zones, maintaining a presence in all 16 hotspots. The organization has deployed 3,469 additional community mobilizers and expanded its response to include more than 590,000 litres of safe water, support to health facilities and schools, and decontamination capacity. UNICEF requires US$ 113.5 million for its revised six-month response plan, with US$ 41 million mobilized as of 30 September, leaving a US$ 72.5 million funding gap. Timely, flexible funding is urgently needed to sustain outbreak control and protect vulnerable populations, including children, in areas affected by conflict and displacement.
Original source text (English)
Skip to main content Help Log in ReliefWeb Search Content Search What are you looking for? Search|t Updates Countries Disasters Organizations Topics Jobs Training Informing humanitarians worldwide 24/7 — a service provided by UN OCHA DR Congo UNICEF Democratic Republic of the Congo Humanitarian Situation Report No. 4 – Ebola Outbreak (Bundibugyo Virus), 30 September Format Situation Report Source UNICEF Posted 27 Oct 2026 Originally published 27 Oct 2026 Origin View original Attachments Download Report (PDF | 1.01 MB) HIGHLIGHTS Children continued to bear a disproportionate share of the outbreak. As of 30 September, children accounted for approximately 27 per cent of confirmed cases but nearly 38 per cent of deaths, with children under five representing almost two-thirds of child deaths. In response to the outbreak’s geographic expansion, UNICEF scaled up its multisectoral operations from 60 to 63 health zones, maintaining a presence in all 16 hotspots. Community-based surveillance was strengthened through the deployment of 3,469 additional community mobilizers. Risk Communication and Community Engagement (RCCE) activities expanded to 55 affected health zones, mobilizing 11,513 frontline and community actors, extending collaboration from 108 to 114 radio stations, and launching intensified digital engagement and rumour management. Infection Prevention and Control (IPC) and Water, Sanitation and Hygiene (WASH) interventions transitioned from initial deployment to implementation at scale. UNICEF delivered more than 590,000 litres of safe water, expanded support to health facilities and schools, strengthened decontamination capacity, and deployed assistance to newly affected areas. With the start of the school year, 167,343 children and adolescents accessed safe and adapted learning opportunities, while 158,188 unique children received school or IPC/WASH kits. UNICEF requires US$ 113.5 million for its revised six-month response plan. As of 30 September, US$ 41 million had been mobilized, leaving a US$ 72.5 million funding gap. Timely, flexible funding is urgently required to sustain outbreak control, protect children and maintain essential health including mental health and psychosocial support, nutrition, education, IPC/WASH and protection services in communities already affected by conflict, displacement and cholera. EPIDEMIOLOGICAL SITUATION UPDATE Between 1 and 30 September 2026, the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) reported 2,050 additional confirmed Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) cases and 979 deaths. As of 30 September, cumulative confirmed cases had reached 8,300, including 4,018 deaths, corresponding to a case fatality ratio (CFR) of 48.4 per cent. The outbreak continued to expand geographically, with confirmed cases reported for the first time in Sud-Ubangi Province, bringing the number of affected provinces to seven (Ituri, North Kivu, South Kivu, Haut-Uélé, Tshopo, Bas-Uélé and South-Ubangi). During September, an additional three health zones reported confirmed cases, increasing the total to 63 affected health zones, representing 37.7 per cent of the country's 167 health zones. 16 health zones across North Kivu, Ituri and Haut-Uélé provinces were identified as Ebola hotspots, highlighting the scale of transmission and the need for increased prevention, surveillance and response measures. UNICEF maintained an operational presence across all 16 hotspot health zones, delivering services in support of the Ebola response. The outbreak is now the largest Ebola outbreak ever recorded in the DRC and the second largest globally. Ituri province remains the epicentre, accounting for 75.8 per cent of all confirmed cases 6,293 cases and 2,902 deaths, followed by North Kivu 1,601 cases and 950 deaths and Haut-Uélé 346 cases and 144 deaths. Transmission also continued in Tshopo, Bas-Uélé, South Kivu and South-Ubangi. Women and girls represent approximately 53.4 per cent of reported confirmed cases. Children aged 17 years and below account for approximately 27.4 per cent of confirmed cases and 37.6 per cent of reported deaths, indicating a disproportionate mortality burden among children. Children under five bear the greatest burden, representing approximately 63.8 per cent of deaths among children aged 17 years and below. During September, 16 health workers were identified among confirmed cases reported during the month, underscoring ongoing risks to frontline responders. South Sudan conducts a full-scale simulation exercise to test Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) readiness - WHO | Regional Office for Africa

News Brief
On 26 October 2026, the World Health Organization (WHO) Regional Office for Africa reported that South Sudan conducted a full-scale simulation exercise to test readiness for Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD). This exercise is part of WHO's broader efforts to strengthen health systems and respond to health emergencies in the African Region. The simulation aimed to improve preparedness and response capabilities, but no confirmed cases, deaths, or exposures were reported in the supplied evidence. The event highlights the importance of proactive measures in combating potential outbreaks of BVD, which is a member of the Ebola virus family. The response status indicates that the exercise was part of a larger initiative to enhance public health readiness, with no further details provided on its outcomes or ongoing actions.

WHO scales up Bundibugyo virus disease readiness in the Eastern Mediterranean Region amid conflict and displacement - World Health Organization (WHO)
新闻简报尽管存在冲突和流离失所,世界卫生组织(WHO)仍在吉布提、索马里和苏丹加强对本迪布焦病毒病(BVD)的防备。尚未确认 BVD 病例,但由于卫生系统不堪重负,风险很高。世卫组织正在提供培训、技术支持和医疗用品,以加强 监测、实验室能力和感染预防和控制措施。
